## HUM_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AHUM_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

物种类别对你的帝国没有太大影响，但与其他类别相比，同是人形类的物种，双方认同感会更强。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/name_lists/name_lists_l_simp_chinese.yml:6`

### English

Species Class has no effect on your Empire, but Humanoid-class Species may consider other Humanoids to be less alien than other Species Classes.

Source: `localisation/english/name_lists/name_lists_l_english.yml:9`

## MAM_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMAM_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

物种类别对你的帝国没有太大影响，但与其他类别相比，同是哺乳类的物种，双方认同感会更强。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/name_lists/name_lists_l_simp_chinese.yml:39`

### English

Species Class has no effect on your Empire, but Mammalian-class Species may consider other Mammalians to be less alien than other Species Classes.

Source: `localisation/english/name_lists/name_lists_l_english.yml:43`

## NECROID_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ANECROID_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

物种类别对你的帝国没有太大影响，但与其他类别相比，同是死灵类的物种，双方认同感会更强。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/name_lists/name_lists_l_simp_chinese.yml:543`

### English

Species Class has no effect on your Empire, but Necroid-class Species may consider other Necroids to be less alien than other Species Classes.

Source: `localisation/english/name_lists/name_lists_l_english.yml:566`

## AQUATIC_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AAQUATIC_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

物种类别对你的帝国没有太大影响，但与其他类别相比，同是水生类的物种，双方认同感会更强。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/name_lists/name_lists_l_simp_chinese.yml:599`

### English

Species Class has no effect on your Empire, but Aquatic-class Species may consider other Aquatics to be less alien than other Species Classes.

Source: `localisation/english/name_lists/name_lists_l_english.yml:625`

## TOX_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATOX_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

物种类别对你的帝国没有太大影响，但与其他类别相比，同是毒物类的物种，双方认同感会更强。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/name_lists/name_lists_l_simp_chinese.yml:675`

### English

Species Class has no effect on your Empire, but Toxoid-class Species may consider other Toxoids to be less alien than other Species Classes.

Source: `localisation/english/name_lists/name_lists_l_english.yml:703`

## INF_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AINF_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

物种类别对你的帝国没有太大影响，但与其他类别相比，同是$INF$类的物种，双方认同感会更强。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/name_lists/name_lists_l_simp_chinese.yml:743`

### English

Species Class has no effect on your Empire, but $INF$-class Species may consider other $INF_plural$ to be less alien than other Species Classes.

Source: `localisation/english/name_lists/name_lists_l_english.yml:774`

## CYBERNETIC_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ACYBERNETIC_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

物种类别对你的帝国没有太大影响，但与其他类别相比，同是义体类的物种，双方认同感会更强。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/name_lists/name_lists_l_simp_chinese.yml:850`

### English

Species Class has no effect on your Empire, but Cybernetic-class Species may consider other Cybernetics to be less alien than other Species Classes.

Source: `localisation/english/name_lists/name_lists_l_english.yml:886`
